<div class="question" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

The original price of a bicycle is $\pounds 260$. Which of these is the better deal?

- Deal A: Take $25 \%$ off any purchase!
- Deal B: Sale Price $\pounds 205$ 

![papers/aldenham-school/2019/sample-paper-1/s01-q10-i00.png](/assets/papers/aldenham-school/2019/sample-paper-1/s01-q10-i00.png "papers/aldenham-school/2019/sample-paper-1/s01-q10-i00.png")

![papers/aldenham-school/2019/sample-paper-1/s01-q10-i01.png](/assets/papers/aldenham-school/2019/sample-paper-1/s01-q10-i01.png "papers/aldenham-school/2019/sample-paper-1/s01-q10-i01.png")

Show workings. 

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">The original price of a bicycle is $\pounds 260$. Which of these is the better deal?
<emptyline>
- Deal A: Take $25 \%$ off any purchase!
- Deal B: Sale Price $\pounds 205$ 
<emptyline>
%image%
<emptyline>
%image%
<emptyline>
Show workings. 
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

<div class="tip">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">
- ABBR: $\text {Sale price = Original price} - \text{Discount } \% \times \text{Original price}$

- ABBR: $\text {Sale price = (100 - Discount})\ \% \times \text{Original price}$
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

Let's calculate the sale price of bicycle from each deal.

**Deal A**

If the price is reduced by $25 \%$, then the sale price will be $100 - 25 = 75 \%$ of the original price. Using that, we get

$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Sale price from Deal A}     &= 75 \% \ \text{of} \ 260 \\\\
                                  &= \dfrac {75} {100} \times 260   \\\\
                                  &= \dfrac {75 \times 260 } {100}  \\\\
                                  &= \dfrac {25 \times 3 \times 4 \times 65 } {25 \times 4}  \\\\
                                  &= \dfrac {\cancel{25} \times 3 \times \cancel{4} \times 65 } {\cancel{25}  \times \cancel{4}}   \\\\
                                  &= 3 \times 65 \\\\
                                  &= \pounds 195
\end{aligned}
$

**Deal B**

$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Sale price from Deal B}     &= \pounds 205
\end{aligned}
$

Better deal $= \text{Deal A} = \pounds 195$
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

Deal A

</div>
</div>

</div>

<emptyline>

                                  &= \dfrac {75 \times 260 } {100}  \\\\

                                  &= \dfrac {\cancel{25} \times 3 \times \cancel{4} \times 65 } {\cancel{25}  \times \cancel{4}}   \\\\
                                  &= 3 \times 65 \\\\
                                  &= \pounds 195
\end{aligned}
$
<emptyline>
**Deal B**
<emptyline>
$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Sale price from Deal B}     &= \pounds 205
\end{aligned}
$
<emptyline>

</code></pre>

</div>

<div class="question" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

Zain wants to buy two chairs.  Three shops advertise the same luxury chair.  Each shop has a special offer.

**Shop A**
- Chair: Normal Price $\pounds 600$
- Special Offer: $30 \%$ off Normal Price.

**Shop B**
- Chair: Normal Price $\pounds 550$
- Special Offer: $\dfrac{1}{5}$ off Normal Price.

**Shop C**
- Chair: Normal Price $\pounds 820$
- Special Offer: Buy one Get one free.

![papers/aldenham-school/sample/sample-paper-2/s01-q26-i00.png](/assets/papers/aldenham-school/sample/sample-paper-2/s01-q26-i00.png "papers/aldenham-school/sample/sample-paper-2/s01-q26-i00.png")
![papers/aldenham-school/sample/sample-paper-2/s01-q26-i01.png](/assets/papers/aldenham-school/sample/sample-paper-2/s01-q26-i01.png "papers/aldenham-school/sample/sample-paper-2/s01-q26-i01.png")
![papers/aldenham-school/sample/sample-paper-2/s01-q26-i02.png](/assets/papers/aldenham-school/sample/sample-paper-2/s01-q26-i02.png "papers/aldenham-school/sample/sample-paper-2/s01-q26-i02.png")
![papers/aldenham-school/sample/sample-paper-2/s01-q26-i03.png](/assets/papers/aldenham-school/sample/sample-paper-2/s01-q26-i03.png "papers/aldenham-school/sample/sample-paper-2/s01-q26-i03.png")

At which shop is the price of the two chairs the cheapest? 

You **must** show your working.

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">Zain wants to buy two chairs.  Three shops advertise the same luxury chair.  Each shop has a special offer.
<emptyline>
**Shop A**
- Chair: Normal Price $\pounds 600$
- Special Offer: $30 \%$ off Normal Price.
<emptyline>
**Shop B**
- Chair: Normal Price $\pounds 550$
- Special Offer: $\dfrac{1}{5}$ off Normal Price.
<emptyline>
**Shop C**
- Chair: Normal Price $\pounds 820$
- Special Offer: Buy one Get one free.
<emptyline>
%image%
%image%
%image%
%image%
<emptyline>
At which shop is the price of the two chairs the cheapest? 
<emptyline>
You **must** show your working.
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

<div class="tip">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">
- ABBR: $\text {Sale price = Original price} - \text{Discount } \% \times \text{Original price}$

- ABBR: $\text {Sale price = (100 - Discount})\ \% \times \text{Original price}$
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

Let's calculate the cheapest price of $2$ chairs from each shop.

**Shop A**

If the price is reduced by $30 \%$, then the sale price will be $100 - 30 = 70 \%$ of the original price. Using that, we get

$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Cost of 2 chairs from Shop A}     &= 70\% \times 600 \times 2 \\\\
                                        &= \dfrac{70}{100}  \times 600 \times 2  \\\\
                                        &= \dfrac{70 \times 600 \times 2}{100} \\\\
                                        &= \dfrac{70 \times 6 \cancel{0} \cancel{0} \times 2}{1\cancel{0}\cancel{0}} \\\\
                                        &= 70 \times 6 \times 2 \\\\
                                        &= \pounds 840
\end{aligned}
$

**Shop B**

If the price is reduced by $\dfrac{1}{5}$, then the sale price will be $1 - \dfrac{1}{5} = \dfrac{4}{5}$ of the original price. Using that, we get

$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Cost of } 2 \text{ chairs from Shop B}    &= \dfrac{4}{5} \times 550 \times 2 \\\\
                                                &= \dfrac{4 \times 550 \times 2}{5} \\\\
                                                &= \dfrac{4 \times 5 \times 110 \times 2}{5} \\\\
                                                &= \dfrac{4 \times \cancel{5} \times 110 \times 2}{\cancel{5}} \\\\
                                                &= 4 \times 110 \times 2 \\\\
                                                &= \pounds 880
\end{aligned}
$

**Shop C**

$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Cost of } 2 \text{ chairs from Shop C}     &= \pounds 820
\end{aligned}
$

Cheapest price $= \text{Shop C} = \pounds 820$
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$\text{Shop C} = \pounds 820$

</div>
</div>

$
<emptyline>
**Shop B**

\text{Cost of } 2 \text{ chairs from Shop B}    &= \dfrac{4}{5} \times 550 \times 2 \\\\

                                                &= 4 \times 110 \times 2 \\\\
                                                &= \pounds 880

$
<emptyline>

<div class="question" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

$200$ items are bought for $\pounds 2.10$. Each are now worth $\pounds 1.61$. Find the total loss.

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">$200$ items are bought for $\pounds 2.10$. Each are now worth $\pounds 1.61$. Find the total loss.
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

<div class="tip">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Loss for 1 item}     &= 2.10 - 1.61 \\\\
                           &= 0.49 \\\\
\text{Loss for 200 items}  &= 0.49 \times 200 \\\\
                           &= \pounds 98
\end{aligned}
$
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$\pounds 98$

</div>
</div>

<div class="papername">
<p>aldenham-school--11-plus--maths--9999--sample-paper-4:1:28</p>

<div class="rag">

<div class="rag">

<div class="rag">

<div class="topics">
<ul>
<li>
tprofit
</li>
<li>
tmoney
</li>

<div class="question" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

Have a look at the deals ticket vendors are offering: 

Each ticket cost $\pounds 480$. 

|     Offers    |                                     |
|:-------------:|:-----------------------------------:|
| Super Tickets |         Buy $2$ get $1$ free        |
|  Tickets R US | Buy $3$ get $25 \%$ off total price |
| Ticket Galore |    Buy $1$ get $2$ at $40 \%$ off   |
|   Top Ticket  |      Buy $2$ get $1$ half price     |

![papers/aldenham-school/sample/sample-paper-4/s01-q28-i00.png](/assets/papers/aldenham-school/sample/sample-paper-4/s01-q28-i00.png "papers/aldenham-school/sample/sample-paper-4/s01-q28-i00.png")

If I needed $3$ tickets, put the vendors in order (cheapest first).

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">Have a look at the deals ticket vendors are offering: 
<emptyline>
Each ticket cost $\pounds 480$. 
<emptyline>
|     Offers    |                                     |
|:-------------:|:-----------------------------------:|
| Super Tickets |         Buy $2$ get $1$ free        |
|  Tickets R US | Buy $3$ get $25 \%$ off total price |
| Ticket Galore |    Buy $1$ get $2$ at $40 \%$ off   |
|   Top Ticket  |      Buy $2$ get $1$ half price     |
<emptyline>
%image%
<emptyline>
If I needed $3$ tickets, put the vendors in order (cheapest first).
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

<div class="tip">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

Let's calculate the best price of $3$ tickets from each vendor.

**Super Tickets**

The third ticket is free when you buy $2$ tickets. So, the cost of $3$ tickets is just the cost of $2$ tickets.

$
\begin  {aligned}
\text{Cost of } 3 \text{ tickets}     &= 480 \times 2 \\\\
                                      &= \pounds 960
\end{aligned}
$

**Tickets R US**

If the total price is reduced by $25 \%$, then the sale price will be $100 - 25 = 75 \%$ of the original price. Using that, we get

$
\begin{aligned}
\text {Cost of} \ 3 \ \text{tickets}       &= 75\% \times 480 \times 3 \\\\
                                           &= \dfrac{75}{100} \times 480 \times 3 \\\\
                                           &= \dfrac{75 \times 480 \times 3}{100} \\\\
                                           &= \dfrac{25 \times 3 \times 4 \times 120 \times 3}{25 \times 4} \\\\
                                           &= \dfrac{\cancel{25} \times 3 \times \cancel{4} \times 120 \times 3}{\cancel{25} \times \cancel{4}} \\\\
                                           &= 3 \times 120 \times 3 \\\\
                                           &= \pounds 1080
\end{aligned}
$

**Ticket Galore**

If the price is reduced by $40 \%$, then the sale price will be $100 - 40 = 60 \%$ of the original price. Using that, we get

$
\begin{aligned}
\text {Cost of} \ 3 \ \text{tickets}      &= 480 + 60\% \times 480 \times 2 \\\\
                                          &= 480 + \dfrac{60}{100} \times 480 \times 2 \\\\
                                          &= 480 + \dfrac{60 \times 480 \times 2}{100} \\\\
                                          &= 480 + \dfrac{6\cancel{0} \times 48\cancel{0} \times 2}{1\cancel{0}\cancel{0}} \\\\
                                          &= 480 + 6 \times 48 \times 2 \\\\
                                          &= 480 + 576 \\\\
                                          &= \pounds 1056
\end{aligned}
$

**Top Ticket**

If the price is reduced by $\dfrac{1}{2}$, then the sale price will be $1 - \dfrac{1}{2} = \dfrac{1}{2}$ of the original price. Using that, we get

$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Cost of} \ 3 \ \text{tickets}      &= 480 \times 2 + \dfrac{1}{2} \times 480 \\\\
                                         &= 960 + \dfrac{480}{2} \\\\
                                         &= 960 + \dfrac{240 \times 2}{2} \\\\
                                         &= 960 + \dfrac{240 \times \cancel{2}}{\cancel{2}} \\\\
                                         &= 960 + 240 \\\\
                                         &= \pounds 1200
\end{aligned}
$

Using the calculation above, the vendors in order (cheapest first):

|  Vendor       | Price          |
|:-------------:|:--------------:|
| Super Tickets | $\pounds 960$  |
| Ticket Galore | $\pounds 1056$ |
| Tickets R US  | $\pounds 1080$ |
| Top Ticket    | $\pounds 1200$ |
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

- Super Tickets
- Ticket Galore
- Tickets R US
- Top Ticket

</div>
</div>
<div class="workingscodeblock">
<div class="tipcodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="tipcodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Tips Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">%empty%
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="formulacodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="formulacodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Formulas Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">%empty%
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workingcodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="workingcodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Working Raw</strong>
</div>

<emptyline>

                                           &= \dfrac{75 \times 480 \times 3}{100} \\\\
                                           &= \dfrac{25 \times 3 \times 4 \times 120 \times 3}{25 \times 4} \\\\
                                           &= \dfrac{\cancel{25} \times 3 \times \cancel{4} \times 120 \times 3}{\cancel{25} \times \cancel{4}} \\\\
                                           &= 3 \times 120 \times 3 \\\\

                                          &= 480 + \dfrac{60 \times 480 \times 2}{100} \\\\
                                          &= 480 + \dfrac{6\cancel{0} \times 48\cancel{0} \times 2}{1\cancel{0}\cancel{0}} \\\\
                                          &= 480 + 6 \times 48 \times 2 \\\\

                                         &= 960 + \dfrac{240 \times \cancel{2}}{\cancel{2}} \\\\

                                         &= \pounds 1200
\end{aligned}
$
<emptyline>
Using the calculation above, the vendors in order (cheapest first):
<emptyline>

|:-------------:|:--------------:|

| Ticket Galore | $\pounds 1056$ |
| Tickets R US  | $\pounds 1080$ |
| Top Ticket    | $\pounds 1200$ |
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answercodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

<div class="question" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

In $2018$ a charity raffle raised $ \pounds 480$. In $2019$ they raised $30 \%$ more. How much did the charity raise in $2019?$

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">In $2018$ a charity raffle raised $ \pounds 480$. In $2019$ they raised $30 \%$ more. How much did the charity raise in $2019?$
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

<div class="tip">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Amount raised in 2019}  &= (100 + 30)\% \text{ of } 480 \\\\
                              &= \dfrac{130}{100} \times 480 \\\\
                              &= \dfrac{130 \times 480}{100} \\\\
                              &= \dfrac{13 \cancel{0} \times 48 \cancel{0} }{\cancel{100}} \\\\
                              &= 13 \times 48 \\\\
                              &= \pounds 624
\end{aligned}
$
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$\pounds 624$

</div>
</div>

**Question**

Clarissa buys the sunglasses and a sun hat. 
How much change, in pounds, does she get from a $\pounds 10$ note?

![papers/bancrofts-school/2013/sample-paper-1/s01-q10-s01-i00.png](/assets/papers/bancrofts-school/2013/sample-paper-1/s01-q10-s01-i00.png "papers/bancrofts-school/2013/sample-paper-1/s01-q10-s01-i00.png")

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">Clarissa buys the sunglasses and a sun hat. 
How much change, in pounds, does she get from a $\pounds 10$ note?
<emptyline>
%image%
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">

</div>

<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

$

                            &= 10 - 9.14 \\\\

\end{aligned}
$

<li class="questionContainer">
<div class="question_envelope rag_not_found subquestion">
<div class="question subquestion" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

Jo and Aisha bought identical jeans from a market stall. 

Jo got $10 \%$ discount of the full price. 

Aisha got $15 \%$ discount of the full price. Jo paid $\pounds 1.20$ more than Aisha. 

What was the full price of the jeans?

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">Jo and Aisha bought identical jeans from a market stall. 
<emptyline>
Jo got $10 \%$ discount of the full price. 
<emptyline>
Aisha got $15 \%$ discount of the full price. Jo paid $\pounds 1.20$ more than Aisha. 
<emptyline>
What was the full price of the jeans?
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

<div class="tip">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

Let the full price of the jeans $= p$

Jo got $10 \%$ discount and Aisha got $15 \%$ discount. So Jo paid $15 - 10 = 5 \%$ more of the full price.

As per the question,

$
\begin{aligned}
5 \% \ \text{of} \ p        &= 1.20 \\\\
\dfrac {5} {100} \times p   &= 1.20 \\\\
p                           &= \dfrac {1.20 \times 100} {5} \\\\
p                           &= \dfrac {1.20 \times 20 \times 5} {5} \\\\
p                           &= \dfrac {1.20 \times 20 \times \cancel{5}} {\cancel{5}} \\\\
p                           &= 1.20 \times 20 \\\\
p                           &= \pounds 24
\end{aligned}
$

Full price of the jeans $= \pounds 24$
</div>
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

<div class="tip">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">
- ABBR: $\text {Sale price = Original price} - \text{Discount } \% \times \text{Original price}$

- ABBR: $\text {Sale price = (100 - Discount})\ \% \times \text{Original price}$
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

Let the full price of the jeans $= p$

As per the question,

$
\begin{aligned}
(p - 10 \% \ \text{of} \ p)  - (p - 15 \% \ \text{of} \ p)  &= 1.20 \\\\
p - 10 \% \ \text{of} \ p  - p + 15 \% \ \text{of} \ p      &= 1.20 \\\\
15 \% \ \text{of} \ p - 10 \% \ \text{of} \ p               &= 1.20 \\\\
5 \% \ \text{of} \ p                                        &= 1.20 \\\\
\dfrac {5} {100} \times p                                   &= 1.20 \\\\
p                                                           &= \dfrac {1.20 \times 100} {5} \\\\
p                                                           &= \dfrac {1.20 \times 20 \times 5} {5} \\\\
p                                                           &= \dfrac {1.20 \times 20 \times \cancel{5}} {\cancel{5}} \\\\
p                                                           &= 1.20 \times 20 \\\\
p                                                           &= \pounds 24
\end{aligned}
$

Full price of the jeans $= \pounds 24$
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$\pounds 24$

</div>
</div>
<div class="workingscodeblock">
<div class="tipcodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="tipcodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Tips Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">%empty%
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="formulacodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="formulacodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Formulas Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">%empty%
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workingcodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="workingcodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Working Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">Let the full price of the jeans $= p$
<emptyline>
Jo got $10 \%$ discount and Aisha got $15 \%$ discount. So Jo paid $15 - 10 = 5 \%$ more of the full price.
<emptyline>
As per the question,
<emptyline>
$
\begin{aligned}
5 \% \ \text{of} \ p        &= 1.20 \\\\
\dfrac {5} {100} \times p   &= 1.20 \\\\
p                           &= \dfrac {1.20 \times 100} {5} \\\\
p                           &= \dfrac {1.20 \times 20 \times 5} {5} \\\\
p                           &= \dfrac {1.20 \times 20 \times \cancel{5}} {\cancel{5}} \\\\
p                           &= 1.20 \times 20 \\\\
p                           &= \pounds 24
\end{aligned}
$
<emptyline>
Full price of the jeans $= \pounds 24$
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="tipcodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="tipcodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Tips Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">%empty%
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="formulacodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="formulacodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Formulas Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">- rtq_abbr_formula_md_profit_loss_selling_price_when_percentage_discount_long
- rtq_abbr_formula_md_profit_loss_selling_price_when_percentage_discount_short
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workingcodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="workingcodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Working Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">Let the full price of the jeans $= p$
<emptyline>
As per the question,
<emptyline>
$
\begin{aligned}
(p - 10 \% \ \text{of} \ p)  - (p - 15 \% \ \text{of} \ p)  &= 1.20 \\\\
p - 10 \% \ \text{of} \ p  - p + 15 \% \ \text{of} \ p      &= 1.20 \\\\
15 \% \ \text{of} \ p - 10 \% \ \text{of} \ p               &= 1.20 \\\\
5 \% \ \text{of} \ p                                        &= 1.20 \\\\
\dfrac {5} {100} \times p                                   &= 1.20 \\\\
p                                                           &= \dfrac {1.20 \times 100} {5} \\\\
p                                                           &= \dfrac {1.20 \times 20 \times 5} {5} \\\\
p                                                           &= \dfrac {1.20 \times 20 \times \cancel{5}} {\cancel{5}} \\\\
p                                                           &= 1.20 \times 20 \\\\
p                                                           &= \pounds 24
\end{aligned}
$
<emptyline>
Full price of the jeans $= \pounds 24$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>

**Question**

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">

\end{aligned}
$

<div class="answercodeblock_title">

<ul class="subquestion lower-alpha question_type_subquestion list_type_lower-alpha">
<li class="questionContainer">
<div class="question_envelope rag_not_found subquestion">
<div class="question subquestion" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

Ashok buys the coat for sale at '$40\%$ off'. How much does he pay for it?

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">Ashok buys the coat for sale at '$40\%$ off'. How much does he pay for it?
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

<div class="tip">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">
- ABBR: $\text {Sale price = (100 - Discount})\ \% \times \text{Original price}$
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

If the price is reduced by $40 \%$, then the new price will be $100 - 40 = 60 \%$ of the original price. Using that, we get

$
\begin{aligned}
\text {Sale price}          &= 60 \% \text{ of } 120 \\\\
                            &= \dfrac {60} {100} \times 120 \\\\
                            &= \dfrac {60 \times 120} {100}  \\\\
                            &= \dfrac {6 \cancel 0 \times 12 \cancel 0} {1 \cancel 0 \cancel 0}  \\\\
                            &= 6 \times 12 \\\\
                            &= \pounds 72
\end{aligned}
$
</div>
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

<div class="tip">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">
- ABBR: $\text {Sale price = Original price} - \text{Discount } \% \times \text{Original price}$
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Sale price}  &= 120 - 40 \% \text{ of }  120  \\\\
                   &= 120 - \dfrac {40} {100} \times 120 \\\\
                   &= 120 - \dfrac {40 \times 120} {100} \\\\
                   &= 120 - \dfrac {4 \cancel 0 \times 12 \cancel 0} {1 \cancel 0 \cancel 0} \\\\
                   &= 120 - 4 \times 12 \\\\
                   &= 120 - 48 \\\\
                   &= \pounds 72
\end{aligned}
$
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$\pounds 72$

</div>
</div>
<div class="workingscodeblock">
<div class="tipcodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="tipcodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Tips Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">%empty%
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="formulacodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="formulacodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Formulas Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">- rtq_abbr_formula_md_profit_loss_selling_price_when_percentage_discount_short
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workingcodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="workingcodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Working Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">If the price is reduced by $40 \%$, then the new price will be $100 - 40 = 60 \%$ of the original price. Using that, we get
<emptyline>
$
\begin{aligned}
\text {Sale price}          &= 60 \% \text{ of } 120 \\\\
                            &= \dfrac {60} {100} \times 120 \\\\
                            &= \dfrac {60 \times 120} {100}  \\\\
                            &= \dfrac {6 \cancel 0 \times 12 \cancel 0} {1 \cancel 0 \cancel 0}  \\\\
                            &= 6 \times 12 \\\\
                            &= \pounds 72
\end{aligned}
$
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="tipcodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="tipcodeblock_title">

</code></pre>

<div class="formulacodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">$

<div class="question_envelope rag_not_found subquestion">
<div class="question subquestion" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

Ed finds the $\pounds 120$ coat at a different website, adverised with a $30\%$ discount. As a 'registered member' he is able to get a further $10\%$ off this advertised price.

Work out the advertised price: 

Reduce the advertised price by $10\%$. How much does Ed pay?

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">Ed finds the $\pounds 120$ coat at a different website, adverised with a $30\%$ discount. As a 'registered member' he is able to get a further $10\%$ off this advertised price.
<emptyline>
Work out the advertised price: 
<emptyline>
Reduce the advertised price by $10\%$. How much does Ed pay?
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

<div class="tip">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

If the price is reduced by $30 \%$, then the advertised price will be $100 - 30 = 70 \%$ of the original price. Using that, we get

$
\begin{aligned}
\text {Advertised price}    &= 70 \% \text{ of } 120 \\\\
                            &= \dfrac {70} {100} \times 120 \\\\
                            &= \dfrac {70 \times 120} {100}  \\\\
                            &= \dfrac {7 \cancel 0 \times 12 \cancel 0} {1 \cancel 0 \cancel 0}  \\\\
                            &= 7 \times 12 \\\\
                            &= \pounds 84
\end{aligned}
$

If the price is reduced by $10 \%$, then the registered member price will be $100 - 10 = 90 \%$ of the advertised price. Using that, we get

$
\begin{aligned}
\text {Member price}    &= 90 \% \text{ of }  84 \\\\
                        &= \dfrac {90} {100} \times 84 \\\\
                        &= \dfrac {90 \times 84} {100}  \\\\
                        &= \dfrac {7560} {100}  \\\\
                        &= \pounds 75.6
\end{aligned}
$
</div>
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

<div class="tip">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Advertised price}     &= \text {Original price} - \text {Discount} \\\\
                            &= 120 - 30 \% \text{ of }  120  \\\\
                            &= 120 - \dfrac {30} {100} \times 120 \\\\
                            &= 120 - \dfrac {30 \times 120} {100} \\\\
                            &= 120 - \dfrac {3 \cancel 0 \times 12 \cancel 0} {1 \cancel 0 \cancel 0} \\\\
                            &= 120 - 3 \times 12 \\\\
                            &= 120 - 36 \\\\
                            &= \pounds 84 \\\\
\text{Member price}         &= \text {Advertised price} - \text {Discount} \\\\
                            &= 84 - 10 \% \text{ of }  120  \\\\
                            &= 84 - \dfrac {10} {100} \times 84 \\\\
                            &= 84 - \dfrac {10 \times 84} {100} \\\\
                            &= 84 - \dfrac {840} {100} \\\\
                            &= 84 - 8.4 \\\\
                            &= \pounds 75.6
\end{aligned}
$
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

Advertised price $=$ $\pounds 84$

</div>
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

Member price $=$ $\pounds 75.6$

</div>
</div>

<div class="question" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

The original cost of a mobile phone is $\pounds 76.30$. The price is reduced by $\pounds 18.45$.

What is the new price?

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">

                    &= \pounds 57.85
\end{aligned}
$
</div>

$\pounds 57.85$

<div class="question" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

A car costing $\pounds 45000$ decreases in value by $15\%$ in one year.

What is its new value?

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">A car costing $\pounds 45000$ decreases in value by $15\%$ in one year.
<emptyline>
What is its new value?
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

<div class="tip">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

If the price is reduced by $15 \%$, then the new price will be $100 - 15 = 85 \%$ of the original price. Using that, we get

$
\begin{aligned}
\text{New value}     &= 85 \% \text{ of } 45000 \\\\
                     &= \dfrac {85} {100} \times 45000   \\\\
                     &= \dfrac {85 \times 45000} {100}  \\\\
                     &= \dfrac {85 \times 450 \cancel {00} } {1 \cancel {00} }  \\\\
                     &= 85 \times 450 \\\\
                     &= \pounds 38250
\end{aligned}
$
</div>
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

<div class="tip">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

$
\begin{aligned}
\text{New price}  &= \text {Old price} - \text {Discount} \\\\
                   &= 45000 - 15 \% \text{ of } 45000  \\\\
                   &= 45000 - \dfrac {15} {100} \times 45000 \\\\
                   &= 45000 - \dfrac {15 \times 45000} {100} \\\\
                   &= 45000 - \dfrac {15 \times 450 \cancel {00}} {1 \cancel {00}} \\\\
                   &= 45000 - 15 \times 450 \\\\
                   &= 45000 - 6750 \\\\
                   &= \pounds 38250
\end{aligned}
$
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$\pounds 38250$

</div>
</div>
<div class="workingscodeblock">
<div class="tipcodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

</div>

</code></pre>
</div>

<div class="question" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

A car costing $\pounds 50000$ decreases in value by $35\%$ in one year.  

What is its new value?

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">A car costing $\pounds 50000$ decreases in value by $35\%$ in one year.  
<emptyline>
What is its new value?
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

<div class="tip">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

If the price is reduced by $35 \%$, then the new price will be $100 - 35 = 65 \%$ of the original price. Using that, we get

$
\begin{aligned}
\text {New price}           &= 65 \% \text{ of } 50000 \\\\
                            &= \dfrac {65} {100} \times 50000 \\\\
                            &= \dfrac {65 \times 50000} {100}  \\\\
                            &= \dfrac {65 \times 500 \cancel {00}} {1 \cancel {00}}  \\\\
                            &= 65 \times 500 \\\\
                            &= \pounds 32500
\end{aligned}
$
</div>
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

<div class="tip">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

$
\begin{aligned}
\text{New price}    &= \text {Old price} - \text {Discount} \\\\
                    &= 50000 - 35 \% \text{ of } 50000  \\\\
                    &= 50000 - \dfrac {35} {100} \times 50000 \\\\
                    &= 50000 - \dfrac {35 \times 50000} {100} \\\\
                    &= 50000 - \dfrac {35 \times 500 \cancel {00}} {1 \cancel {00}} \\\\
                    &= 50000 - 35 \times 500 \\\\
                    &= 50000 - 17500 \\\\
                    &= \pounds 32500
\end{aligned}
$
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$\pounds 32500$

</div>
</div>

<div class="question" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

A shop sells a television for $\pounds 300$. They increase the price by $15\%$.

What is the new price of the television?

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">A shop sells a television for $\pounds 300$. They increase the price by $15\%$.
<emptyline>
What is the new price of the television?
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

<div class="tip">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

If the price is increased by $15 \%$, then the new price will be $100 + 15 = 115 \%$ of the original price. Using that, we get

$
\begin{aligned}
\text {New price}           &= 115 \% \text{ of } 300 \\\\
                            &= \dfrac {115} {100} \times 300 \\\\
                            &= \dfrac {115 \times 300} {100}  \\\\
                            &= \dfrac {115 \times 3 \cancel {00}} {1 \cancel {00}}  \\\\
                            &= 115 \times 3 \\\\
                            &= \pounds 345
\end{aligned}
$
</div>
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

<div class="tip">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Sale price}  &= \text {Original price} + \text {Increment} \\\\
                   &= 300 + 15 \% \text{ of }  300  \\\\
                   &= 300 + \dfrac {15} {100} \times 300 \\\\
                   &= 300 + \dfrac {15 \times 300} {100} \\\\
                   &= 300 + \dfrac {15 \times 3 \cancel {00}} {1 \cancel {00}} \\\\
                   &= 300 + 15 \times 3 \\\\
                   &= 300 + 45 \\\\
                   &= \pounds 345
\end{aligned}
$
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$\pounds 345$

</div>
</div>

<div class="tipcodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

                            &= 115 \times 3 \\\\

<div class="question" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

A chocolate bar costs $\pounds 0.45$. A pack containing $5$ of the bars costs $\pounds 1.90$.

If you buy $5$ bars how much money do you save by buying the $5$ pack?

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">A chocolate bar costs $\pounds 0.45$. A pack containing $5$ of the bars costs $\pounds 1.90$.
<emptyline>
If you buy $5$ bars how much money do you save by buying the $5$ pack?
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

<div class="tip">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Money saved}    &= 5 \times 0.45 - 1.90 \\\\
                      &= 2.25 - 1.90 \\\\
                      &= \pounds 0.35
\end{aligned}
$
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$\pounds 0.35$

</div>
</div>

**Question**

                    &= \pounds 161.35

$

</div>

<div class="question" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

A car costing $\pounds 50{,}000$ decreases in value by $15 \%$ in one year.

What is its new value?

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">A car costing $\pounds 50{,}000$ decreases in value by $15 \%$ in one year.
<emptyline>
What is its new value?
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

<div class="tip">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

If the price is reduced by $15 \%$, then the new price will be $100 - 15 = 85 \%$ of the original price. Using that, we get

$
\begin{aligned}
\text {Sale price}          &= 85 \% \text{ of } 50000 \\\\
                            &= \dfrac {85}{100} \times 50000 \\\\
                            &= \dfrac {85 \times 50000}{100}  \\\\
                            &= \dfrac {85 \times 500 \cancel {00}}{1 \cancel {00}}  \\\\
                            &= 85 \times 500 \\\\
                            &= \pounds 42500
\end{aligned}
$
</div>
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

<div class="tip">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">
- ABBR: $\text {Sale price = Original price} - \text{Discount } \% \times \text{Original price}$
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Sale price}  &= 50000 - 15 \% \text{ of } 50000  \\\\
                   &= 50000 - \dfrac {15} {100} \times 50000 \\\\
                   &= 50000 - \dfrac {15 \times 50000} {100} \\\\
                   &= 50000 - \dfrac {15 \times 500 \cancel {00}} {1 \cancel {00}} \\\\
                   &= 50000 - 15 \times 500 \\\\
                   &= 50000 - 7500 \\\\
                   &= \pounds 42500
\end{aligned}
$
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$\pounds 42500$

</div>
</div>
<div class="workingscodeblock">
<div class="tipcodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="tipcodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Tips Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">%empty%
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="formulacodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="formulacodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Formulas Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">%empty%
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workingcodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="workingcodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Working Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">If the price is reduced by $15 \%$, then the new price will be $100 - 15 = 85 \%$ of the original price. Using that, we get
<emptyline>
$
\begin{aligned}
\text {Sale price}          &= 85 \% \text{ of } 50000 \\\\
                            &= \dfrac {85}{100} \times 50000 \\\\
                            &= \dfrac {85 \times 50000}{100}  \\\\
                            &= \dfrac {85 \times 500 \cancel {00}}{1 \cancel {00}}  \\\\
                            &= 85 \times 500 \\\\
                            &= \pounds 42500
\end{aligned}
$
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="tipcodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="tipcodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Tips Raw</strong>

<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">%empty%
</code></pre>

<div class="formulacodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Formulas Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">- rtq_abbr_formula_md_profit_loss_selling_price_when_percentage_discount_long
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workingcodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="workingcodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Working Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Sale price}  &= 50000 - 15 \% \text{ of } 50000  \\\\
                   &= 50000 - \dfrac {15} {100} \times 50000 \\\\
                   &= 50000 - \dfrac {15 \times 50000} {100} \\\\
                   &= 50000 - \dfrac {15 \times 500 \cancel {00}} {1 \cancel {00}} \\\\
                   &= 50000 - 15 \times 500 \\\\
                   &= 50000 - 7500 \\\\
                   &= \pounds 42500
\end{aligned}
$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>

<div class="question" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

A coat cost $\pounds 125$ and was then reduced by $20\%$ in a sale. What is the new price?

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">A coat cost $\pounds 125$ and was then reduced by $20\%$ in a sale. What is the new price?
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

<div class="tip">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

As per the question,

$
\begin{aligned}
\text {New price}       &= \text {Original cost} - \text {Sale Discount} \\\\
                        &= \pounds 125 - 20 \% \ \text {of} \ \pounds 125 \\\\
                        &= 125 - \dfrac {20} {100} \times 125 \\\\
                        &= 125 - \dfrac {20 \times 125} {100} \\\\
                        &= 125 - \dfrac {20 \times 5 \times 25} {20 \times 5} \\\\
                        &= 125 - \dfrac {\cancel {20} \times \cancel {5} \times 25} {\cancel {20} \times \cancel {5}} \\\\
                        &= 125 - 25 \\\\
                        &= \pounds 100
\end{aligned}
$
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$\pounds 100$

</div>
</div>

</div>

</code></pre>
</div>

</div>

                        &= 125 - \dfrac {20 \times 125} {100} \\\\

</div>
</div>

<div class="question" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

The supermarket had a sale on their bottles of lemonade. Instead of selling them for $80 \ \text{p}$ per bottle, 
they sold them for $55 \ \text{p}$ per bottle.
They sold $200$ bottles of lemonade in a week.
How much less did they take by selling at the lower price?  

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">The supermarket had a sale on their bottles of lemonade. Instead of selling them for $80 \ \text{p}$ per bottle, 
they sold them for $55 \ \text{p}$ per bottle.
They sold $200$ bottles of lemonade in a week.
How much less did they take by selling at the lower price?  
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

<div class="tip">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Required amount}    &= 200 \times (80 - 55) \\\\
                          &= 200 \times 25 \\\\
                          &= 5000 \ \text{p} \\\\
                          &= \pounds 50
\end{aligned}
$
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$\pounds 50$

</div>
</div>

<div class="tipcodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

<div class="question" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

The full price of a car is $\pounds 12000$. The car is in a sale and its price has been reduced by $35\%$. 

Monique is saving up to buy the car. She already has $\pounds 7350$. How much more money does she need to buy the car? 

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">The full price of a car is $\pounds 12000$. The car is in a sale and its price has been reduced by $35\%$. 
<emptyline>
Monique is saving up to buy the car. She already has $\pounds 7350$. How much more money does she need to buy the car? 
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

<div class="tip">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

If the price is reduced by $35 \%$, then the sale price will be $100 - 35 = 65 \%$ of the original price. Using that we get,

$
\begin{aligned}
\text {New price}               &= 65 \% \text { of } 12000 \\\\
                                &= \dfrac {65} {100} \times 12000 \\\\
                                &= \dfrac {65 \times 12000} {100} \\\\
                                &= \dfrac {65 \times 120 \cancel {00}} {1 \cancel {00}} \\\\
                                &= 65 \times 120 \\\\
                                &= \pounds 7800 \\\\
\text {Remaining money needed}  &= 7800 - 7350 \\\\
                                &= \pounds 450
\end{aligned}
$
</div>
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

<div class="tip">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

$
\begin{aligned}
\text{New price}                &= \text {Original price} - \text {Discount} \\\\
                                &= 12000 - 35 \% \text{ of }  12000  \\\\
                                &= 12000 - \dfrac {35} {100} \times 12000 \\\\
                                &= 12000 - \dfrac {35 \times 12000} {100} \\\\
                                &= 12000 - \dfrac {35 \times 120 \cancel {00}} {1 \cancel {00}} \\\\
                                &= 12000 - 35 \times 120 \\\\
                                &= 12000 - 4200 \\\\
                                &= \pounds 7800 \\\\
\text {Remaining money needed}  &= 7800 - 7350 \\\\
                                &= \pounds 450
\end{aligned}
$
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$\pounds 450$

</div>
</div>

<div class="question" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

A book usually costs $\pounds 18.60$ but it is reduced by $15\%$.How much does it cost now? 

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">A book usually costs $\pounds 18.60$ but it is reduced by $15\%$.How much does it cost now? 
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

<div class="tip">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

If the price is reduced by $15 \%$, then the sale price will be $100 - 15 = 85 \%$ of the original price. Using that we get,

$
\begin{aligned}
\text {New price}         &= 85 \% \text { of } 18.60 \\\\
                          &= \dfrac {85} {100} \times 18.6 \\\\
                          &= \dfrac {85 \times 18.6} {100} \\\\
                          &= \dfrac {1581} {100} \\\\
                          &= \pounds 15.81
\end{aligned}
$
</div>
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

<div class="tip">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

$
\begin{aligned}
\text{New price}            &= \text {Original price} - \text {Discount} \\\\
                            &= 18.60 - 15 \% \text{ of } 18.60  \\\\
                            &= 18.60 - \dfrac {15} {100} \times 18.60 \\\\
                            &= 18.60 - \dfrac {15 \times 18.60} {100} \\\\
                            &= 18.60 - \dfrac {279} {100} \\\\
                            &= 18.60 - 2.79 \\\\
                            &= \pounds 15.81
\end{aligned}
$
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$\pounds 15.81$

</div>
</div>

<div class="question" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

A bicycle at Store $P$ costs $\text{\textdollar} 200$. The regular price of the same bicycle at Store $Q$ is $15\%$ more than it is at Store P. The bicycle is on sale at Store Q for $10\%$ off of the regular price.

What is the sale price of the bicycle at Store $Q?$ 

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">A bicycle at Store $P$ costs $\text{\textdollar} 200$. The regular price of the same bicycle at Store $Q$ is $15\%$ more than it is at Store P. The bicycle is on sale at Store Q for $10\%$ off of the regular price.
<emptyline>
What is the sale price of the bicycle at Store $Q?$ 
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

<div class="tip">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

If the price is increased by $15 \%$, then the sale price will be $100 + 15 = 115 \%$ of the original price. Using that we get,

$
\begin{aligned}
\text {Regular price at store Q}    &= 115 \% \text { of } 200 \\\\
                                    &= \dfrac {115} {100} \times 200 \\\\
                                    &= \dfrac {115 \times 200} {100} \\\\
                                    &= \dfrac {115 \times 2 \cancel {00}} {1 \cancel {00}} \\\\
                                    &= 115 \times 2 \\\\
                                    &= \pounds 230
\end{aligned}
$

If the price is decreased by $10 \%$, then the sale price will be $100 - 10 = 90 \%$ of the original price. Using that we get,

$
\begin{aligned}
\text {Sale price at store Q}       &= 90 \% \text { of } 230 \\\\
                                    &= \dfrac {90} {100} \times 230 \\\\
                                    &= \dfrac {90 \times 230} {100} \\\\
                                    &= \dfrac {9 \cancel 0 \times 23 \cancel 0} {1 \cancel 0 \cancel 0} \\\\
                                    &= 9 \times 23 \\\\
                                    &= \pounds 207
\end{aligned}
$
</div>
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

<div class="tip">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Regular price at store Q}   &= 200 + 15 \% \text{ of } 200 \\\\
                                  &= 200 + \dfrac {15 \times 200} {100} \\\\
                                  &= 200 + \dfrac {15 \times 2 \cancel {00}} {1 \cancel {00}} \\\\
                                  &= 200 + 15 \times 2 \\\\
                                  &= 200 + 30 \\\\
                                  &= \pounds 230 \\\\
\text{Sale price at store Q}      &= 230 - 10 \% \text{ of } 230 \\\\
                                  &= 230 - \dfrac {10 \times 230} {100} \\\\
                                  &= 230 - \dfrac {1 \cancel 0 \times 23 \cancel 0} {1 \cancel {00}} \\\\
                                  &= 230 - 23 \\\\
                                  &= \pounds 207
\end{aligned}
$
</div>
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

<div class="tip">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Discounted price at store Q}      &= 90 \% \text{ of } 115 \% \text{ of } 200 \\\\
                                        &= \dfrac {90} {100} \times \dfrac {115} {100} \times 200 \\\\
                                        &= \dfrac {90 \times 115 \times 200} {100 \times 100} \\\\
                                        &= \dfrac {9 \times 10 \times 5 \times 23 \times 2 \times 100} {2 \times 5 \times 10 \times 100} \\\\
                                        &= \dfrac {9 \times \cancel {10} \times \cancel 5 \times 23 \times \cancel 2 \times \cancel {100}} {\cancel 2 \times \cancel 5 \times \cancel {10} \times \cancel {100}} \\\\
                                        &= 9 \times 23 \\\\
                                        &= \pounds 207
\end{aligned}
$
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$\pounds 207$

</div>
</div>

**Question**

The normal price of a TV was $\pounds 600$. Work out the sale price of the TV.

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

</div>

<div class="formulas placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

If the price is reduced by $35 \%$, then the new price will be $100 - 35 = 65 \%$ of the original price. Using that, we get

$
\begin{aligned}
\text {Sale price}  &= 65\% \ \text{of} \ 600 \\\\
                    &= \dfrac {65} {100} \times 600 \\\\
                    &= \dfrac {65 \times 600} {100} \\\\
                    &= \dfrac {65 \times 6 \cancel {00}} {1 \cancel {00}} \\\\
                    &= 65 \times 6 \\\\
                    &= \pounds 390
\end{aligned}
$
</div>
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

<div class="tip">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">
- ABBR: $\text {Sale price = Original price} - \text{Discount } \% \times \text{Original price}$
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

$

\text {Sale price}  &= 600  - 35\% \ \text{of} \ 600 \\\\
                    &= 600 - \dfrac {35} {100} \times 600 \\\\
                    &= 600 - \dfrac {35 \times 600} {100} \\\\
                    &= 600 - \dfrac {35 \times 6 \cancel {00}} {1 \cancel {00}} \\\\
                    &= 600 - 35 \times 6 \\\\
                    &= 600 - 210 \\\\
                    &= \pounds 390
\end{aligned}
$
</div>
</div>

**Question**

The normal price of a clock was reduced by $\pounds 140$. Work out the normal price of the clock.

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">The normal price of a clock was reduced by $\pounds 140$. Work out the normal price of the clock.
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

Let the normal price $= a$.

As per the question,

$
\begin{aligned}
35\% \times a                &= 140 \\\\
\dfrac {35} {100} \times a   &= 140 \\\\
a                            &= \dfrac {140 \times 100} {35} \\\\
a                            &= \dfrac {35 \times 4 \times 100} {35} \\\\
a                            &= \dfrac {\cancel {35} \times 4 \times 100} {\cancel {35}} \\\\
a                            &= 4 \times 100 \\\\
a                            &= \pounds 400
\end{aligned}
$

Normal price $= \pounds 400$
</div>
</div>

<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

The sale price of a computer was $\pounds 884$. Work out the normal price of the computer.

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">The sale price of a computer was $\pounds 884$. Work out the normal price of the computer.
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

<div class="tip">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

Let the normal price of $= a$.

If the price is reduced by $35\%$, then the new price will be $100 - 35 = 65\%$ of the original price. Using that, we get

As per the question,

$ 
\begin{aligned}
65 \% \times a                    &= 884 \\\\
\dfrac {65} {100} \times a        &= 884 \\\\
a                                 &= \dfrac {884 \times 100} {65} \\\\
a                                 &= \dfrac {13 \times 68 \times 5 \times 20} {13 \times 5} \\\\
a                                 &= \dfrac {\cancel {13} \times 68 \times \cancel 5 \times 20} {\cancel {13} \times \cancel 5} \\\\
a                                 &= 68 \times 20 \\\\
a                                 &= \pounds 1360
\end{aligned}
$

Normal price of a computer $= \pounds 1360$
</div>
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

As per the question,

$ 
\begin{aligned}
a - 35 \% \times a                                  &= 884 \\\\
a - \dfrac {35} {100} \times a                      &= 884 \\\\
a - \dfrac {35a} {100}                              &= 884 \\\\
\dfrac {100 \times a} {100} - \dfrac {35a} {100}    &= 884 \\\\
\dfrac {100a} {100} - \dfrac {35a} {100}            &= 884 \\\\
\dfrac {100a - 35a} {100}                           &= 884 \\\\
\dfrac {65a} {100}                                  &= 884 \\\\
a                                                   &= \dfrac {884 \times 100} {65} \\\\
a                                                   &= \dfrac {13 \times 68 \times 5 \times 20} {13 \times 5} \\\\
a                                                   &= \dfrac {\cancel {13} \times 68 \times \cancel 5 \times 20} {\cancel {13} \times \cancel 5} \\\\
a                                                   &= 68 \times 20 \\\\
a                                                   &= \pounds 1360
\end{aligned}
$

Normal price of a computer $= \pounds 1360$
</div>
</div>

<div class="question" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

Bella is given $\pounds 50$ for her birthday. She spends $\pounds 37.50$ on clothes. She then goes to the cinema with a friend, but as they have a $2$ for $1$ voucher, they only spend $\pounds 5.40$ between the two of them which they both pay half. She buys some popcorn and a drink for $\pounds 3.20$.

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

How much money does Bella have left?

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">How much money does Bella have left?
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
