</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Money left}       &= 50 - 37.50 - \dfrac {5.40} {2} - 3.2 \\\\
                        &= 50 - 37.50 - 2.70 - 3.20 \\\\
                        &= 50 - 43.40 \\\\
                        &= \pounds 6.60
\end{aligned}
$
</div>

<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

<div class="question_envelope rag_not_found subquestion">
<div class="question subquestion" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

Bella's friend wants to go out for supper. Bella knows that she needs to save $\pounds 2.50$ for the bus home. Assuming Bella and her friend both pay an equal amount; can Bella afford any of the food deals below? If so, which one? 

DEAL $1$: Buy one meal for $\pounds 7$, get the second half price. 

DEAL $2$: $10\%$ off all dishes costing $\pounds 5$ or more. 

DEAL $3$: Buy one get one free on all dishes costing $\pounds 8$ or more.

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">Bella's friend wants to go out for supper. Bella knows that she needs to save $\pounds 2.50$ for the bus home. Assuming Bella and her friend both pay an equal amount; can Bella afford any of the food deals below? If so, which one? 
<emptyline>
DEAL $1$: Buy one meal for $\pounds 7$, get the second half price. 
<emptyline>
DEAL $2$: $10\%$ off all dishes costing $\pounds 5$ or more. 
<emptyline>
DEAL $3$: Buy one get one free on all dishes costing $\pounds 8$ or more.
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

<div class="tip">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Money for food}             &= 6.6 - 2.5 \\\\
                                  &= \pounds 4.1 \\\\
\text{Money for food for both}    &= 2 \times 4.1 \\\\
                                  &= \pounds 8.2 \\\\
\text{Price for Deal 1}           &= 7 + \dfrac {7} {2} \\\\
                                  &= 7 + 3.5 \\\\
                                  &= \pounds 10.5 \\\\
\text{Price for Deal 2}           &= 2 \times \left( 5 - \dfrac {10} {100} \times 5 \right) \\\\
                                  &= 2 \times \left( 5 - \dfrac {10 \times 5} {100} \right) \\\\
                                  &= 2 \times \left( 5 - \dfrac {50} {100} \right) \\\\
                                  &= 2 \times (5 - 0.5) \\\\
                                  &= 2 \times 4.5 \\\\
                                  &= \pounds 9 \\\\
\text{Price of Deal 3}            &= \pounds 8
\end{aligned}
$

$Deal 3$ is affordable for Bella.
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

Deal $3$

</div>
</div>

                                  &= \pounds 4.1 \\\\

                                  &= \pounds 8.2 \\\\
\text{Price for Deal 1}           &= 7 + \dfrac {7} {2} \\\\
                                  &= 7 + 3.5 \\\\
                                  &= \pounds 10.5 \\\\
\text{Price for Deal 2}           &= 2 \times \left( 5 - \dfrac {10} {100} \times 5 \right) \\\\
                                  &= 2 \times \left( 5 - \dfrac {10 \times 5} {100} \right) \\\\
                                  &= 2 \times \left( 5 - \dfrac {50} {100} \right) \\\\
                                  &= 2 \times (5 - 0.5) \\\\
                                  &= 2 \times 4.5 \\\\
                                  &= \pounds 9 \\\\
\text{Price of Deal 3}            &= \pounds 8
\end{aligned}
$

$Deal 3$ is affordable for Bella.

<div class="rag">

<div class="question" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

Seesaws cost $\pounds 200$ each in January $2010$. In January $2011$ they had gone up by $45\%$. Calculate how much a seesaw cost in January $2011$.

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">Seesaws cost $\pounds 200$ each in January $2010$. In January $2011$ they had gone up by $45\%$. Calculate how much a seesaw cost in January $2011$.
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

<div class="tip">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

If the price has gone up by $45 \%$, then the new price will be $100 + 45 = 145 \%$ of the original price. Using that, we get

$
\begin{aligned}
\text {Price of seesaw in} \ 2011         &= 145 \% \times 200 \\\\
                                          &= \dfrac {145}{100} \times 200 \\\\
                                          &= \dfrac {145 \times 200}{100}  \\\\
                                          &= \dfrac {145 \times 2 \cancel{0} \cancel{0}}{1 \cancel{0} \cancel{0}}  \\\\
                                          &= 145 \times 2  \\\\
                                          &= \pounds 290
\end{aligned}
$
</div>
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

<div class="tip">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

$
\begin{aligned}
\text{New price}            &= \text {Original price} - \text {Discount} \\\\
                            &= 200 + 45 \% \text{ of } 200  \\\\
                            &= 200 + \dfrac {45} {100} \times 200 \\\\
                            &= 200 + \dfrac {45 \times 200} {100} \\\\
                            &= 200 + \dfrac {45 \times 2 \cancel {00}} {1 \cancel {00}} \\\\
                            &= 200 + 45 \times 2 \\\\
                            &= 200 + 90 \\\\
                            &= \pounds 290
\end{aligned}
$
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$\pounds 290$

</div>
</div>
<div class="workingscodeblock">
<div class="tipcodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="tipcodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Tips Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">%empty%
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="formulacodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="formulacodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Formulas Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">%empty%
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workingcodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="workingcodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Working Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">If the price has gone up by $45 \%$, then the new price will be $100 + 45 = 145 \%$ of the original price. Using that, we get
<emptyline>
$
\begin{aligned}
\text {Price of seesaw in} \ 2011         &= 145 \% \times 200 \\\\
                                          &= \dfrac {145}{100} \times 200 \\\\
                                          &= \dfrac {145 \times 200}{100}  \\\\
                                          &= \dfrac {145 \times 2 \cancel{0} \cancel{0}}{1 \cancel{0} \cancel{0}}  \\\\
                                          &= 145 \times 2  \\\\
                                          &= \pounds 290
\end{aligned}
$
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="tipcodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="tipcodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Tips Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">%empty%
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="formulacodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="formulacodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Formulas Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">%empty%
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workingcodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="workingcodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Working Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">$
\begin{aligned}
\text{New price}            &= \text {Original price} - \text {Discount} \\\\
                            &= 200 + 45 \% \text{ of } 200  \\\\
                            &= 200 + \dfrac {45} {100} \times 200 \\\\
                            &= 200 + \dfrac {45 \times 200} {100} \\\\
                            &= 200 + \dfrac {45 \times 2 \cancel {00}} {1 \cancel {00}} \\\\
                            &= 200 + 45 \times 2 \\\\
                            &= 200 + 90 \\\\
                            &= \pounds 290
\end{aligned}
$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answercodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="answercodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Option Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="answercodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="answercodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Key Raw</strong>
</div>

<div class="answercodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="answercodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Answer Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">$\pounds 290$

<div class="question" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

A used-car dealer sells a car at $120 \%$ of its cost. If a car costs $\pounds 25000$, how much will he sell the car for?

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">A used-car dealer sells a car at $120 \%$ of its cost. If a car costs $\pounds 25000$, how much will he sell the car for?
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

<div class="tip">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Selling price}      &= 120 \% \ \text{of} \ 25000 \\\\
                          &= \dfrac {120} {100} \times 25000 \\\\
                          &= \dfrac {120 \times 25000} {100} \\\\
                          &= \dfrac {120 \times 250 \cancel {00}} {1 \cancel {00}} \\\\
                          &= 120 \times 250 \\\\
                          &= \pounds 30000
\end{aligned}
$
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$\pounds 30000$

</div>
</div>

<div class="question" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

A calculator normally costs $\pounds 3.80$. It is offered in a sale at $25\%$ off. What is the sale price?  

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">A calculator normally costs $\pounds 3.80$. It is offered in a sale at $25\%$ off. What is the sale price?  
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

<div class="tip">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

If the price is reduced by $25 \%$, then the new price will be $100 - 25 = 75 \%$ of the original price. Using that, we get

$
\begin{aligned}
\text {Sales price}             &= 75 \% \text { of }  3.80 \\\\
                                &= \dfrac {75} {100} \times 3.80 \\\\
                                &= \dfrac {75 \times 3.80} {100} \\\\
                                &= \dfrac {285} {100} \\\\
                                &= \pounds 2.85
\end{aligned}
$
</div>
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

<div class="tip">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">
- ABBR: $\text {Sale price = Original price} - \text{Discount } \% \times \text{Original price}$
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Sales price}    &= 3.80 - 25 \% \ \text {of} \ 3.80 \\\\
                      &= 3.80 - \dfrac {25} {100} \times 3.80 \\\\
                      &= 3.80 - \dfrac {25 \times 3.80} {100} \\\\
                      &= 3.80 - \dfrac {95} {100} \\\\
                      &= 3.80 - 0.95 \\\\
                      &= \pounds 2.85
\end{aligned}
$
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$\pounds 2.85$

</div>
</div>
<div class="workingscodeblock">
<div class="tipcodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="tipcodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Tips Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">%empty%
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="formulacodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="formulacodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Formulas Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">%empty%
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workingcodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="workingcodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Working Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">If the price is reduced by $25 \%$, then the new price will be $100 - 25 = 75 \%$ of the original price. Using that, we get
<emptyline>
$
\begin{aligned}
\text {Sales price}             &= 75 \% \text { of }  3.80 \\\\
                                &= \dfrac {75} {100} \times 3.80 \\\\
                                &= \dfrac {75 \times 3.80} {100} \\\\
                                &= \dfrac {285} {100} \\\\
                                &= \pounds 2.85
\end{aligned}
$
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="tipcodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="tipcodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Tips Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">%empty%
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="formulacodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="formulacodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Formulas Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">- rtq_abbr_formula_md_profit_loss_selling_price_when_percentage_discount_long
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workingcodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="workingcodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Working Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Sales price}    &= 3.80 - 25 \% \ \text {of} \ 3.80 \\\\
                      &= 3.80 - \dfrac {25} {100} \times 3.80 \\\\
                      &= 3.80 - \dfrac {25 \times 3.80} {100} \\\\
                      &= 3.80 - \dfrac {95} {100} \\\\
                      &= 3.80 - 0.95 \\\\
                      &= \pounds 2.85
\end{aligned}
$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">

<div class="question" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

A calculator normally costs $\pounds 24.40$. It is offered in a sale at $15\%$ off. What is the sale price? 

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">A calculator normally costs $\pounds 24.40$. It is offered in a sale at $15\%$ off. What is the sale price? 
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

<div class="tip">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

If the price is reduced by $15 \%$, then the new price will be $100 - 15 = 85 \%$ of the original price. Using that, we get

$
\begin{aligned}
\text {Sale price}          &= 85 \% \times 24.40 \\\\
                            &= \dfrac {85}{100} \times 24.40 \\\\
                            &= \dfrac {85 \times 24.40}{100}  \\\\
                            &= \dfrac {2074}{100}  \\\\
                            &= \pounds 20.74
\end{aligned}
$
</div>
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

<div class="tip">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">
- ABBR: $\text {Sale price = Original price} - \text{Discount } \% \times \text{Original price}$
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Sale price}  &= 24.40 - 15 \% \times 24.40  \\\\
                   &= 24.40 - \dfrac {15} {100} \times 24.40 \\\\
                   &= 24.40 - \dfrac {15 \times 24.40} {100} \\\\
                   &= 24.40 - \dfrac {366} {100} \\\\
                   &= 24.40 - 3.66 \\\\
                   &= \pounds 20.74
\end{aligned}
$
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$\pounds 20.74$

</div>
</div>

</div>

</code></pre>

<div class="question" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

Bill sold his motor scooter to Tom for $\pounds 120$. After driving it for a few days, Tom discovered it was in such a broken-down condition that he sold it back to Bill for $30\%$ 
less than he paid. The next day Bill sold it to Jack for $\pounds 90$. What is Bill's profit on the final sale?

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">Bill sold his motor scooter to Tom for $\pounds 120$. After driving it for a few days, Tom discovered it was in such a broken-down condition that he sold it back to Bill for $30\%$ 
less than he paid. The next day Bill sold it to Jack for $\pounds 90$. What is Bill's profit on the final sale?
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

<div class="tip">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Total amount paid to Bill}    &= 120 + 90 \\\\
                                    &= \pounds 210 \\\\
\text{Amount paid by Bill}          &= 120 - 30 \% \ \text{of} \ 120 \\\\
                                    &= 120 - \dfrac {30} {100} \times 120 \\\\
                                    &= 120 - \dfrac {30 \times 120} {100} \\\\
                                    &= 120 - \dfrac {3 \cancel 0 \times 12 \cancel 0} {1 \cancel 0 \cancel 0} \\\\
                                    &= 120 - 3 \times 12 \\\\
                                    &= 120 - 36 \\\\
                                    &= \pounds 84 \\\\
\text{Bill's profit}                &= 210 - 84 \\\\
                                    &= \pounds 126
\end{aligned}
$
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$\pounds 126$

</div>
</div>
<div class="workingscodeblock">
<div class="tipcodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="tipcodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Tips Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">%empty%
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="formulacodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="formulacodeblock_title">

</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">%empty%
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workingcodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="workingcodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Working Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Total amount paid to Bill}    &= 120 + 90 \\\\
                                    &= \pounds 210 \\\\
\text{Amount paid by Bill}          &= 120 - 30 \% \ \text{of} \ 120 \\\\
                                    &= 120 - \dfrac {30} {100} \times 120 \\\\
                                    &= 120 - \dfrac {30 \times 120} {100} \\\\
                                    &= 120 - \dfrac {3 \cancel 0 \times 12 \cancel 0} {1 \cancel 0 \cancel 0} \\\\
                                    &= 120 - 3 \times 12 \\\\
                                    &= 120 - 36 \\\\
                                    &= \pounds 84 \\\\
\text{Bill's profit}                &= 210 - 84 \\\\
                                    &= \pounds 126
\end{aligned}
$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>

<div class="question" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

There is a sale at the local mobile phone shop. 
Alex wants to buy a new phone which originally cost $\pounds 150$. 
The prices of all the phones have been reduced by $20 \%$.

What amount will Alex have to pay for his new phone?

- $\pounds 135.00$
- $\pounds 149.70$
- $\pounds 147.00$
- $\pounds 130.00$
- $\pounds 120.00$  

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">There is a sale at the local mobile phone shop. 
Alex wants to buy a new phone which originally cost $\pounds 150$. 
The prices of all the phones have been reduced by $20 \%$.
<emptyline>
What amount will Alex have to pay for his new phone?
<emptyline>
- $\pounds 135.00$
- $\pounds 149.70$
- $\pounds 147.00$
- $\pounds 130.00$
- $\pounds 120.00$  
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

<div class="tip">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

If the price is reduced by $20 \%$, then the new price will be $100 - 20 = 80 \%$ of the original price. Using that, we get

$
\begin{aligned} 
\text{Sale price}       &= 80 \% \text{ of } 150 \\\\
                        &= \dfrac {80} {100} \times 150  \\\\
                        &= \dfrac {80 \times 150} {100} \\\\
                        &= \dfrac {8 \cancel{0} \times 15 \cancel{0}} {1 \cancel{0} \cancel{0}} \\\\
                        &= 8 \times 15 \\\\
                        &= \pounds 120
\end{aligned}
$
</div>
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

<div class="tip">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

$
\begin{aligned}
\text{New price}            &= \text {Original price} - \text {Discount} \\\\
                            &= 150 - 20 \% \text{ of } 150  \\\\
                            &= 150 - \dfrac {20} {100} \times 150 \\\\
                            &= 150 - \dfrac {20 \times 150} {100} \\\\
                            &= 150 - \dfrac {2 \cancel 0 \times 15 \cancel 0} {1 \cancel 0 \cancel 0} \\\\
                            &= 150 - 2 \times 15 \\\\
                            &= 150 - 30 \\\\
                            &= \pounds 120
\end{aligned}
$
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="option" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Option**

<p>E</p>
</div>
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$\pounds 120$ 

</div>
</div>

<div class="question_envelope rag_not_found subquestion">
<div class="question subquestion" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

The rate of $\text{VAT}$ (value-added tax) in this country is $20\%$. In the shop CoCost, the price of a TV excluding $\text{VAT}$ is 
$\pounds 550$. Work out the cost of the TV after the $\text{VAT}$ is added on.

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">The rate of $\text{VAT}$ (value-added tax) in this country is $20\%$. In the shop CoCost, the price of a TV excluding $\text{VAT}$ is 
$\pounds 550$. Work out the cost of the TV after the $\text{VAT}$ is added on.
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

<div class="tip">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

If the price is increased by $20 \%$, then the total cost will be $100 + 20 = 120 \%$ of the original price. Using that, we get

$
\begin{aligned}
\text {Total cost}  &= 120 \% \ \text {of} \ 550 \\\\
                    &= \dfrac {120} {100} \times 550 \\\\
                    &= \dfrac {120 \times 550} {100} \\\\
                    &= \dfrac {12 \cancel {0} \times 55 \cancel {0}} {1 \cancel {0} \cancel {0}} \\\\
                    &= 12 \times 55 \\\\
                    &= \pounds 660
\end{aligned}
$
</div>
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

<div class="tip">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

$
\begin{aligned}
\text {Total cost}  &= 550 + 20 \% \ \text {of} \ 550 \\\\
                    &= 550 + \dfrac {20} {100} \times 550 \\\\
                    &= 550 + \dfrac {20 \times 550} {100} \\\\
                    &= 550 + \dfrac {2 \cancel {0} \times 55 \cancel {0}} {1 \cancel {0} \cancel {0}} \\\\
                    &= 550 + 2 \times 55 \\\\
                    &= 550 + 110 \\\\
                    &= \pounds 660
\end{aligned}
$
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$\pounds 660$

</div>
</div>
<div class="workingscodeblock">
<div class="tipcodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="tipcodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Tips Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">%empty%
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="formulacodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="formulacodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Formulas Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">%empty%
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workingcodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="workingcodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Working Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">If the price is increased by $20 \%$, then the total cost will be $100 + 20 = 120 \%$ of the original price. Using that, we get
<emptyline>
$
\begin{aligned}
\text {Total cost}  &= 120 \% \ \text {of} \ 550 \\\\
                    &= \dfrac {120} {100} \times 550 \\\\
                    &= \dfrac {120 \times 550} {100} \\\\
                    &= \dfrac {12 \cancel {0} \times 55 \cancel {0}} {1 \cancel {0} \cancel {0}} \\\\
                    &= 12 \times 55 \\\\
                    &= \pounds 660
\end{aligned}
$
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="tipcodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="tipcodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Tips Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">%empty%
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="formulacodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="formulacodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Formulas Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">%empty%
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workingcodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="workingcodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Working Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">$
\begin{aligned}
\text {Total cost}  &= 550 + 20 \% \ \text {of} \ 550 \\\\
                    &= 550 + \dfrac {20} {100} \times 550 \\\\
                    &= 550 + \dfrac {20 \times 550} {100} \\\\
                    &= 550 + \dfrac {2 \cancel {0} \times 55 \cancel {0}} {1 \cancel {0} \cancel {0}} \\\\
                    &= 550 + 2 \times 55 \\\\
                    &= 550 + 110 \\\\
                    &= \pounds 660
\end{aligned}
$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answercodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

<div class="question subquestion" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

The price of a diamond ring, including $\text{VAT}$ is $\pounds 1200$. Work out the cost of the ring before the $\text{VAT}$ was added on. 

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">The price of a diamond ring, including $\text{VAT}$ is $\pounds 1200$. Work out the cost of the ring before the $\text{VAT}$ was added on. 
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

<div class="tip">
%empty%
</div>
</div>

%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

$
\begin{aligned}
\text {Cost of the ring excluding VAT}  &= 1200 - 20 \% \ \text {of} \ 1200 \\\\
                                        &= 1200 - \dfrac {20} {100} \times 1200 \\\\
                                        &= 1200 - \dfrac {20 \times 1200} {100} \\\\
                                        &= 1200 - \dfrac {20 \times 12 \cancel {00}} {1 \cancel {00}} \\\\
                                        &= 1200 - 20 \times 12 \\\\
                                        &= 1200 - 240 \\\\
                                        &= \pounds 960
\end{aligned}
$
</div>
</div>

<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

<div class="question_envelope rag_not_found subquestion">
<div class="question subquestion" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

The price of a hoodie in the London branch of Abergavenny and Filtch is $\pounds 80$. In New York the price is US$ \ \text{\textdollar}90$. 
How much do I save by buying the hoodie in New York? Give your answer both in US dollars and pounds.

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">The price of a hoodie in the London branch of Abergavenny and Filtch is $\pounds 80$. In New York the price is US$ \ \text{\textdollar}90$. 
How much do I save by buying the hoodie in New York? Give your answer both in US dollars and pounds.
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

<div class="tip">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Price in London}        &= 1.5 \times 80 \\\\
                              &= \text{\textdollar} 120 \\\\
\text{Money saved}            &= 120 - 90 \\\\
                              &= \text{\textdollar} 30 \\\\
\text{Money saved in \pounds} &= \dfrac {30} {1.5} \\\\
                              &= \dfrac {30 \times 10} {15} \\\\
                              &= \dfrac {15 \times 2 \times 10} {15} \\\\
                              &= \dfrac {\cancel {15} \times 2 \times 10} {\cancel {15}} \\\\
                              &= 2 \times 10 \\\\
                              &= \pounds 20
\end{aligned}
$
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$\text{\textdollar} 30, \pounds 20$

</div>
</div>

</code></pre>

<div class="workingcodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Working Raw</strong>

                              &= \text{\textdollar} 120 \\\\

                              &= \dfrac {15 \times 2 \times 10} {15} \\\\

**Question**

My friend Sheila lives in Auckland, New Zealand. She saves US$\ \text{\textdollar}60$ if she buys this hoodie in New York. How much does it cost in New Zealand? Give your answer in New Zealand dollars.

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">My friend Sheila lives in Auckland, New Zealand. She saves US$\ \text{\textdollar}60$ if she buys this hoodie in New York. How much does it cost in New Zealand? Give your answer in New Zealand dollars.
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

<div class="tip">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Profit in \pounds}                    &= \pounds \dfrac {90} {1.5} \\\\
\text{Profit in New Zealand \textdollar}    &= 2 \times \dfrac {90} {1.5} \\\\
                                            &= 2 \times \dfrac {90 \times 10} {15} \\\\
                                            &= \dfrac {2 \times 90 \times 10} {15} \\\\
                                            &= \dfrac {2 \times 15 \times 6 \times 10} {15} \\\\
                                            &= \dfrac {2 \times \cancel {15} \times 6 \times 10} {\cancel {15}} \\\\
                                            &= 2 \times 6 \times 10 \\\\
                                            &= \text{\textdollar} 120
\end{aligned}
$
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$\text{\textdollar} 120$

</div>
</div>

**Question**

Work out the exchange rate for converting New Zealand dollars into American dollars:

$1$ New Zealand dollar (NZ$ \ \text{\textdollar}$) $= \ldots$ American dollars (US$ \ \text{\textdollar}$)

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">Work out the exchange rate for converting New Zealand dollars into American dollars:
<emptyline>
$1$ New Zealand dollar (NZ$ \ \text{\textdollar}$) $= \ldots$ American dollars (US$ \ \text{\textdollar}$)
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

$
\begin{aligned}
\text{1 New Zealand in \textdollar}           &= \text{\textdollar} \dfrac {1} {2} \\\\
\text{\textdollar in New Zealand \textdollar} &= 1.5 \times \dfrac {1} {2} \\\\
                                              &= \dfrac {1.5} {2} \\\\
                                              &= \dfrac {1.5 \times 5} {2 \times 5} \\\\
                                              &= \dfrac {7.5} {10} \\\\
                                              &= 0.75
\end{aligned}
$

$1$ New Zealand dollar \textdollar $= 0.75$ US \textdollar
</div>

<li class="questionContainer">
<div class="question_envelope rag_not_found subquestion">
<div class="question subquestion" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

Work out the sale price of a bed originally costing $\pounds 1600$.

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">Work out the sale price of a bed originally costing $\pounds 1600$.
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

<div class="tip">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

If the price is reduced by $25 \%$, then the sale price would be $100 - 25 = 75 \%$ of the original price.

$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Sale price}               &= 75 \% \ \text{of} \ 1600\\\\
                                &= \dfrac {75} {100} \times 1600 \\\\
                                &= \dfrac {75 \times 1600} {100} \\\\
                                &= \dfrac {75 \times 16 \cancel{00}} {1 \cancel{00}} \\\\
                                &= 75 \times 16 \\\\
                                &= \pounds 1200
\end{aligned}
$
</div>
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

<div class="tip">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Sale price}  &= \text {Original price} - \text {Discount} \\\\
                   &= 1600 - 25 \% \ \text{of} \ 1600 \\\\
                   &= 1600 - \dfrac {25} {100} \times 1600 \\\\
                   &= 1600 - \dfrac {25 \times 1600} {100} \\\\
                   &= 1600 - \dfrac {25 \times 16 \cancel {00}} {1 \cancel {00}} \\\\
                   &= 1600 - 25 \times 16 \\\\
                   &= 1600 - 400 \\\\
                   &= \pounds 1200
\end{aligned}
$
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$\pounds 1200$

</div>
</div>

<div class="workingcodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="workingcodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Working Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Sale price}  &= \text {Original price} - \text {Discount} \\\\
                   &= 1600 - 25 \% \ \text{of} \ 1600 \\\\
                   &= 1600 - \dfrac {25} {100} \times 1600 \\\\
                   &= 1600 - \dfrac {25 \times 1600} {100} \\\\
                   &= 1600 - \dfrac {25 \times 16 \cancel {00}} {1 \cancel {00}} \\\\
                   &= 1600 - 25 \times 16 \\\\
                   &= 1600 - 400 \\\\
                   &= \pounds 1200
\end{aligned}
$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>

<div class="question_envelope rag_not_found subquestion">
<div class="question subquestion" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

Work out the original price of a sofa costing $\pounds 600$ in the sale.

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">Work out the original price of a sofa costing $\pounds 600$ in the sale.
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

<div class="tip">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">
- ABBR: $\text {Sale price = Original price} - \text{Discount } \% \times \text{Original price}$
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

Let the original price $= a$

As per the question,

$
\begin{aligned}
a - 25 \% \times \ a                                        &= 600 \\\\
a - \dfrac {25} {100} \times a                              &= 600 \\\\
a - \dfrac {25 \times a} {100}                              &= 600 \\\\
a - \dfrac {25a} {100}                                      &= 600 \\\\
\dfrac {100 \times a} {100} - \dfrac {25a} {100}            &= 600 \\\\
\dfrac {100a} {100} - \dfrac {25a} {100}                    &= 600 \\\\
\dfrac {100a - 25a} {100}                                   &= 600 \\\\
\dfrac {75a} {100}                                          &= 600 \\\\
a                                                           &= 600 \times \dfrac {100} {75} \\\\
a                                                           &= \dfrac {600 \times 100} {75} \\\\
a                                                           &= \dfrac {75 \times 8 \times 100} {75} \\\\
a                                                           &= \dfrac {\cancel {75} \times 8 \times 100} {\cancel {75}} \\\\
a                                                           &= 8 \times 100 \\\\
a                                                           &= \pounds 800
\end{aligned}
$
</div>
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

</div>
</div>

Let the original price $= a$

As per the question,

$

a                               &= 600 \times \dfrac {100} {75} \\\\
a                               &= \dfrac {600 \times 100} {75} \\\\
a                               &= \dfrac {75 \times 8 \times 100} {75} \\\\
a                               &= \dfrac {\cancel {75} \times 8 \times 100} {\cancel {75}} \\\\
a                               &= 8 \times 100 \\\\
a                               &= \pounds 800
\end{aligned}
$

Original price $= \pounds 800$
</div>
</div>

<li class="questionContainer">
<div class="question_envelope rag_not_found subquestion">
<div class="question subquestion" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

At the end of the month a further reduction of $20\%$ is taken off the sale prices of all items in the store.

-  Anne says that this is the same as an overall reduction of $45\%$ off the original price.
-  Ben says that this gives an overall reduction of less than $45\%$.  
-  Charlie says that this gives an overall reduction of more than $45\%$.  
-  Diana says that the overall percentage reduction varies depending on the original cost of each item.

Who is right?

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">At the end of the month a further reduction of $20\%$ is taken off the sale prices of all items in the store.
<emptyline>
-  Anne says that this is the same as an overall reduction of $45\%$ off the original price.
-  Ben says that this gives an overall reduction of less than $45\%$.  
-  Charlie says that this gives an overall reduction of more than $45\%$.  
-  Diana says that the overall percentage reduction varies depending on the original cost of each item.
<emptyline>
Who is right?
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

<div class="tip">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

Let the original price $= a$

If the price is reduced by $25 \%$, then the sale price would be $100 - 25 = 75 \%$ of the original price.

$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Sale price after first discount}  &= 75 \% \ \text{of} \ a \\\\
                                        &= \dfrac {75} {100} \times a \\\\
                                        &= \dfrac {75 \times a} {100} \\\\
                                        &= \dfrac {25 \times 3 \times a} {25 \times 4} \\\\
                                        &= \dfrac {\cancel {25} \times 3 \times a} {\cancel {25} \times 4} \\\\
                                        &= \dfrac {3 \times a} {4} \\\\
                                        &= \pounds \dfrac {3a} {4}
\end{aligned}
$

If the price is reduced by $20 \%$, then the sale price would be $100 - 20 = 80 \%$ of the original price.

$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Sale price after second discount} &= 80 \% \ \text{of} \ \dfrac {3a} {4} \\\\
                                        &= \dfrac {80} {100} \times \dfrac {3a} {4} \\\\
                                        &= \dfrac {80 \times 3a} {100 \times 4} \\\\
                                        &= \dfrac {20 \times 4 \times 3a} {100 \times 4} \\\\
                                        &= \dfrac {20 \times \cancel 4 \times 3a} {100 \times \cancel 4} \\\\
                                        &= \dfrac {20 \times 3a} {100} \\\\
                                        &= \pounds \dfrac {60a} {100}
\end{aligned}
$

If the price is reduced by $45 \%$, then the sale price would be $100 - 45 = 55 \%$ of the original price.

$
\begin{aligned}
\text{New sale price}       &= 55 \% \ \text{of} \ a \\\\
                            &= \dfrac {55} {100} \times a \\\\
                            &= \dfrac {55 \times a} {100} \\\\
                            &= \pounds \dfrac {55a} {100}
\end{aligned}
$

Ben is right.
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

Ben

</div>
</div>

</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">%empty%
</code></pre>
</div>

</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">%empty%
</code></pre>
</div>

</div>

                                        &= \dfrac {75 \times a} {100} \\\\
                                        &= \dfrac {25 \times 3 \times a} {25 \times 4} \\\\
                                        &= \dfrac {\cancel {25} \times 3 \times a} {\cancel {25} \times 4} \\\\

<div class="question" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

In the American state of Kentucky sales tax is charged at $6\%$.

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">

<li class="questionContainer">
<div class="question_envelope rag_not_found subquestion">
<div class="question subquestion" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

Anila wants to buy a pair of jeans in Kentucky. They are priced at $\text{\textdollar}72$ before allowing for the sales tax. How much does Anila pay for the pair of jeans?

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">Anila wants to buy a pair of jeans in Kentucky. They are priced at $\text{\textdollar}72$ before allowing for the sales tax. How much does Anila pay for the pair of jeans?
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

<div class="tip">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

If the price is increased by $6 \%$, then the new price will be $100 + 6 = 106 \%$ of the original price. Using that, we get

$
\begin{aligned} 
\text{Sale price}       &= 106 \% \text{ of } 72 \\\\
                        &= \dfrac {106} {100} \times 72 \\\\
                        &= \dfrac {106 \times 72} {100} \\\\
                        &= \dfrac {7632} {100} \\\\
                        &= \pounds 76.32
\end{aligned}
$
</div>
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

<div class="tip">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Price}    &= 72 + 6 \% \text{ of } 72 \\\\
                &= 72 + \dfrac {6} {100} \times 72 \\\\
                &= 72 + \dfrac {6 \times 72} {100} \\\\
                &= 72 + \dfrac {432} {100} \\\\
                &= 72 + 4.32 \\\\
                &= \pounds 76.32
\end{aligned}
$
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$\pounds 76.32$

</div>
</div>

                        &= \pounds 76.32

</div>

<li class="questionContainer">
<div class="question_envelope rag_not_found subquestion">
<div class="question subquestion" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

Peter buys a bag of sweets in Kentucky. He pays $\text{\textdollar}4.77$ including sales tax. 
What was the price of sweets before sales tax was added?

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">Peter buys a bag of sweets in Kentucky. He pays $\text{\textdollar}4.77$ including sales tax. 
What was the price of sweets before sales tax was added?
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

<div class="tip">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

Let the price before sales tax $= a$

If the price is increased by $6 \%$, then the new price will be $100 + 6 = 106 \%$ of the original price. Using that, we get

As per the question,

$
\begin{aligned}
106 \% \ \text{of} \ a              &= 4.77 \\\\
\dfrac {106} {100} \times a         &= 4.77 \\\\
a                                   &= \dfrac {4.77 \times 100} {106} \\\\
a                                   &= \dfrac {477 \times 100} {106 \times 100} \\\\
a                                   &= \dfrac {9 \times 53 \times 50 \times 2} {2 \times 53 \times 100} \\\\
a                                   &= \dfrac {9 \times \cancel {53} \times 50 \times \cancel 2} {\cancel 2 \times \cancel {53} \times 100} \\\\
a                                   &= \dfrac {9 \times 50} {100} \\\\
a                                   &= \dfrac {450} {100} \\\\
a                                   &= \pounds 4.50
\end{aligned}
$

Price before the sales tax $= \pounds 4.50$
</div>
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

<div class="tip">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

Let the price before sales tax $= a$

As per the question,

$
\begin{aligned}
a + 6 \% \ \text{of} \ a            &= 4.77 \\\\
a + \dfrac {6} {100} \times a       &= 4.77 \\\\
a + \dfrac {6a} {100}               &= 4.77 \\\\
a + 0.06a                           &= 4.77 \\\\
1.06a                               &= 4.77 \\\\
a                                   &= \dfrac {4.77} {1.06} \\\\
a                                   &= \dfrac {477 \times 100} {106 \times 100} \\\\
a                                   &= \dfrac {9 \times 53 \times 50 \times 2} {2 \times 53 \times 100} \\\\
a                                   &= \dfrac {9 \times \cancel {53} \times 50 \times \cancel 2} {\cancel 2 \times \cancel {53} \times 100} \\\\
a                                   &= \dfrac {9 \times 50} {100} \\\\
a                                   &= \dfrac {450} {100} \\\\
a                                   &= \pounds 4.50
\end{aligned}
$

Price before the sales tax $= \pounds 4.50$
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$\pounds 4.50$

</div>
</div>

<div class="question" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

*"Comfyclothes"* in the Arndale Centre reduces its prices by $20 \%$ in its winter sale. Ian bought a coat for $\pounds 60$ in the sale. 

How much would it have cost before the sale started?

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">*"Comfyclothes"* in the Arndale Centre reduces its prices by $20 \%$ in its winter sale. Ian bought a coat for $\pounds 60$ in the sale. 
<emptyline>
How much would it have cost before the sale started?
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

<div class="tip">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

Let the original price $= p$

If the price is reduced by $20 \%$, then the reduced price will be $100 - 20 = 80 \%$ of the original price. Using that, we get

As per the question,

$
\begin{aligned}
80 \% \ \text{of} \ p           &= 60 \\\\
\dfrac {80} {100} \times p      &= 60 \\\\
p                               &= \dfrac {60 \times 100} {80} \\\\
p                               &= \dfrac {20 \times 3 \times 4 \times 25} {20 \times 4} \\\\
p                               &= \dfrac {\cancel{20} \times 3 \times \cancel 4 \times 25} {\cancel{20} \times \cancel 4} \\\\
p                               &= 3 \times 25 \\\\
p                               &= \pounds 75
\end{aligned}
$

Original price $= \pounds 75$
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$\pounds 75$

</div>
</div>

</code></pre>

$
<emptyline>

<div class="question" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

In the summer sales, prices of items are reduced by $15 \%$. What 
would be the sale price of a coat that originally cost $\pounds 30?$  

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">In the summer sales, prices of items are reduced by $15 \%$. What 
would be the sale price of a coat that originally cost $\pounds 30?$  
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

<div class="tip">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

If the price is reduced by $15 \%$, then the sale price would be $100 - 15 = 85 \%$ of the original price.
Using that we get,

$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Sale price}               &= 85 \% \ \text{of} \ 30\\\\
                                &= \dfrac {85} {100} \times 30 \\\\
                                &= \dfrac {85 \times 30} {100} \\\\
                                &= \dfrac {85 \times 3 \cancel{0}} {10 \cancel{0}} \\\\
                                &= \dfrac {85 \times 3} {10} \\\\
                                &= \dfrac {255} {10} \\\\
                                &= \pounds 25.5
\end{aligned}
$
</div>
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

<div class="tip">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Sale price}  &= \text {Original price} - \text {Discount} \\\\
                   &= 30 - 15 \% \ \text{of} \ 30 \\\\
                   &= 30 - \dfrac {15} {100} \times 30 \\\\
                   &= 30 - \dfrac {15 \times 30} {100} \\\\
                   &= 30 - \dfrac {15 \times 3 \cancel 0} {10 \cancel 0} \\\\
                   &= 30 - \dfrac {15 \times 3} {10} \\\\
                   &= 30 - \dfrac {45} {10} \\\\
                   &= 30 - 4.5 \\\\
                   &= \pounds 25.5
\end{aligned}
$
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$\pounds 25.5$

</div>
</div>
<div class="workingscodeblock">
<div class="tipcodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="tipcodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Tips Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">%empty%
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="formulacodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="formulacodeblock_title">

</code></pre>
</div>

<div class="workingcodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Working Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">If the price is reduced by $15 \%$, then the sale price would be $100 - 15 = 85 \%$ of the original price.
Using that we get,
<emptyline>
$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Sale price}               &= 85 \% \ \text{of} \ 30\\\\
                                &= \dfrac {85} {100} \times 30 \\\\
                                &= \dfrac {85 \times 30} {100} \\\\
                                &= \dfrac {85 \times 3 \cancel{0}} {10 \cancel{0}} \\\\
                                &= \dfrac {85 \times 3} {10} \\\\
                                &= \dfrac {255} {10} \\\\
                                &= \pounds 25.5
\end{aligned}
$
</code></pre>
</div>

                   &= 30 - \dfrac {15 \times 30} {100} \\\\

\end{aligned}
$

<div class="question" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

A shopkeeper buys a box of $60$ apples for $\pounds 12$. 
If he finds that $\dfrac{1}{10}$ of the apples are bad and can't be sold, at what price must he sell each of the good apples so that he makes a total **profit** of $\pounds 15?$

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">A shopkeeper buys a box of $60$ apples for $\pounds 12$. 
If he finds that $\dfrac{1}{10}$ of the apples are bad and can't be sold, at what price must he sell each of the good apples so that he makes a total **profit** of $\pounds 15?$
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

<div class="tip">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

$
\begin{aligned}
\text {Total apples}            &= 60 \\\\
\text {Number of bad apples}    &= \dfrac {1}{10} \times 60  \\\\
                                &= \dfrac {60}{10} \\\\
                                &= \dfrac {6{\cancel{0}}} {1{\cancel{0}}} \\\\
                                &= 6 \\\\
\text {Number of good apples}   &= 60 - 6 \\\\
                                &= 54 \\\\
\text{Total selling price}      &= \text {Profit} + \text {Cost price} \\\\
                                &= 15 + 12 \\\\
                                &= \pounds 27 \\\\
\text {Selling price of each good apple}    &= \dfrac{\text{Total selling price}} {\text{Total good apples}} \\\\
                                            &= \dfrac {27}{54}\\\\
                                            &= \dfrac {27}{27 \times 2}\\\\
                                            &= \dfrac {\cancel {27}}{\cancel {27} \times 2}\\\\
                                            &= \dfrac {1}{2} \\\\
                                            &= \pounds 0.5
\end{aligned}
$
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$\pounds 0.50$

</div>

<div class="question_envelope rag_not_found subquestion">
<div class="question subquestion" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

A tennis racket costs $\pounds 85$ originally. In a sale it is reduced by $15\%$. What is the sale price?

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">A tennis racket costs $\pounds 85$ originally. In a sale it is reduced by $15\%$. What is the sale price?
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

<div class="tip">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

If the price is reduced by $15 \%$, then the sale price would be $100 - 15 = 85 \%$ of the original price.

$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Sale price}               &= 85 \% \ \text{of} \ 85 \\\\
                                &= \dfrac {85} {100} \times 85 \\\\
                                &= \dfrac {85 \times 85} {100} \\\\
                                &= \dfrac {7225} {100} \\\\
                                &= \pounds 72.25
\end{aligned}
$
</div>
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

<div class="tip">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">
- ABBR: $\text {Sale price = Original price} - \text{Discount } \% \times \text{Original price}$
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Sale price}                           &= 85 - 15 \% \ \text{of} \ \pounds 85 \\\\
                                            &= 85 - \dfrac {15} {100} \times 85 \\\\
                                            &= 85 - \dfrac {15 \times 85} {100} \\\\
                                            &= 85 - \dfrac {1275} {100} \\\\
                                            &= 85 - 12.75 \\\\
                                            &= \pounds 72.25
\end{aligned}
$
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$\pounds 72.25$

</div>
</div>

</code></pre>

<li class="questionContainer">
<div class="question_envelope rag_not_found subquestion">
<div class="question subquestion" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

I score $42$ out of $48$ on a test. What percentage is that?

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">I score $42$ out of $48$ on a test. What percentage is that?
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

<div class="tip">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Percentage}       &= \dfrac {42} {48} \times 100 \\\\
                        &= \dfrac {42 \times 100} {48} \\\\
                        &= \dfrac {6 \times 7 \times 25 \times 4} {6 \times 2 \times 4} \\\\
                        &= \dfrac {\cancel 6 \times 7 \times 25 \times \cancel 4} {\cancel 6 \times 2 \times \cancel 4} \\\\
                        &= \dfrac {7 \times 25} {2} \\\\
                        &= \dfrac {175} {2} \\\\
                        &= 87.5 \%
\end{aligned}
$
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$87.5 \%$

</div>
</div>
<div class="workingscodeblock">
<div class="tipcodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="tipcodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Tips Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">%empty%
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="formulacodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="formulacodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Formulas Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">%empty%
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workingcodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="workingcodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Working Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Percentage}       &= \dfrac {42} {48} \times 100 \\\\
                        &= \dfrac {42 \times 100} {48} \\\\
                        &= \dfrac {6 \times 7 \times 25 \times 4} {6 \times 2 \times 4} \\\\
                        &= \dfrac {\cancel 6 \times 7 \times 25 \times \cancel 4} {\cancel 6 \times 2 \times \cancel 4} \\\\
                        &= \dfrac {7 \times 25} {2} \\\\
                        &= \dfrac {175} {2} \\\\
                        &= 87.5 \%
\end{aligned}
$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>
